CORPORATE PROFILE                                                   公司簡介
SHOUGANG FUSHAN RESOURCES GROUP LIMITED                             首鋼福山資源集團有限公司
is one of the most sizable integrated coking coal corporations in   為中國中西部 最具規模之綜合焦煤企業之一。
CENTRAL-WESTERN CHINA.
Taking Shanxi Province as its major investment base, it             以山西省作為主要投資基地，
is principally engaged in MINING OF COKING COAL,                    主要從事 焦煤開採、原焦煤及精焦煤生產及銷
PRODUCTION AND SALES OF RAW AND CLEAN COKING
                                                                    售業務。
COAL.
The Group has three premium operating coking coal mines and         本集團現有三座在產的優質焦煤礦及三座洗煤廠。
three coal preparation plants.

OPERATING MINES                                                        營運中之煤礦

XINGWU COAL MINE                                                       興無煤礦

•   6 km south of Liulin County and the mining right area extended     •   位於柳林縣以南6公里，採礦權區佔地約
    over 11.6 sq. km and spans 4.5 km east to west and 4.5 km              11.6平方公里，東西相距4.5公里，南北相距
    north to south                                                         4.5公里

•   Operation commenced in 1968                                        •   於一九六八年開始營運

•   Annual approved raw coking coal production capacity: 1.75          •   核准年原焦煤生產能力：175萬噸
    million tonnes

•   Operate a coal preparation plant with annual designed input        •   經營一座年入洗量為120萬噸之洗煤廠（於二
    processing capacity of 1.2 million tonnes (put into operation in       零零二年十月投產）
    October 2002)

•   Mainly production of hard coking coal                              •   主要生產硬焦煤

OPERATING MINES (continued)                                               營運中之煤礦（續）

COAL CHARACTERISTICS                                                      煤炭特徵

•     Located within the Lishi-Liulin mining area of Hedong coalfield,    •   位於河東煤田之離柳礦區，該區為中國優質
      one of China’s key reserve areas for high-quality hard coking           硬焦煤之主要儲量區域之一。
      coal.

•     Regarded as “panda coal” because of its scarcity and high           •   由於其稀有性和高經濟價值，被譽為「熊貓
      economic value.                                                         煤」。

•     The Group’s coking coal is of particularly good quality due to      •   本集團之焦煤發熱值高及焦結度強，加上灰
      its high calorific value and caking index with low ash and sulfur       份及硫份低，品質優越，使之非常適合用於
      content characteristics that are highly desirable for coke and          生產焦炭及鋼鐵。
      steel making.

Quality Characteristic                                        主要精焦煤平均質量特徵               基準               參數

half of the year, and prices remained high. In the second half of the
year, the supply gradually eased as imports volume from Mongolia
and Russia increased. The annual coking coal imports volume
increased by 16.7% YoY, combined with falling overseas coking coal
prices, resulting in fluctuated coal prices in the second half of the
year.

         Design         ‧    Prioritize the type of support equipment and mining process that ensures coal quality

          採掘           ‧    根據採掘現場實際情況，制定合理方案保證煤質，並對方案落實情況進行監督

    The Group is committed to safeguarding the safety and health           本集團以保障各利益相關方的安全與健康為
    of all stakeholders. As part of this commitment, the Group             己任。集團不斷細化安全管理架構和制度建
    continually improves its safety management structure and               設，牢築安全生產防線，樹立良好的安全責
    system construction to establish a strong line of defense for          任氛圍，保障員工、承包商的健康與安全。
    workplace safety and a good atmosphere of safety responsibility
    to safeguard the health and safety of its employees and
    contractors.


    5.1 Safety Management System                                           5.1 安全管理體系

        The Group places workplace safety in the forefront of its            本集團將安全生產置於工作首要位置，
        work and, upholds the concept that “safety is employees’             本着「安全是員工最好的福行，安全是

                     ‧   設置公告欄，對有關職業病防治的規章制度、操作規程、職業病危害事故應急救援措施進行及時的宣告

                         hazards exist
                     ‧   When concluding or changing employment contracts with workers, clearly inform them of the occupational

                     ‧   在存在職業病危害的工作場所、作業崗位設置職業病危害告知卡
                     ‧   與勞動者訂立或變更勞動合同時明確告知工作過程中可能產生的職業病危害及其後果、職業病防護措施

     5.3 Workplace Safety Management                                       5.3 安全生產管理

        The Group has always adhered to law-based order and has                 本集團始終堅持將保障生命安全與健康
        made the protection of life safety and health the priority              作為各項安全工作的第一要務，嚴格落
        of all safety work. We strictly implement safety measures               實「一通三防」、應急演練等安全保障措
        such as “One Ventilation and Three Preventions” and                     施，致力為所有相關人員提供健康安全
        emergency drills, and strive to provide a healthy and safe              的工作環境。
        working environment for all relevant personnel.

        Management Measures of “One Ventilation and                            「一通三防」管理舉措
        Three Preventions”

        The Group places importance to mine ventilation, gas                    本集團重視煤礦安全生產中的礦井通
        prevention, coal dust prevention and fire extinguishing                 風、防治瓦斯、防治煤塵、防火滅火
        (“one ventilation and three preventions”) for safe                     （以下簡稱「一通三防」 ）工作，制定《山
        production in the coal mine, and has formulated the                     西福山資源集團有限公司一通三防管理
        Management System of One Ventilation and Three                          制度》《災害預防及處理計劃》 《一通三
        Preventions of Shanxi Fushan Resources Group Limited,                   防各工種操作規程》 《通風設施作業標
        Disaster Prevention and Treatment Plan, Operating                       準》《災害預防與處理計劃》等內部制度
        Procedures for Each Type of Work of One Ventilation and                 文件，規範「一通三防」工作管理標準與
        Three Preventions, Operation Standards for Ventilation                  事故搶險應急處置工作流程。我們建
        Facilities and other internal documents to standardize                  立以總工程師牽頭的「一通三防」管理體
        the management standard of “one ventilation and three                   系，成立一通三防部，根據年度《 「一通
        preventions” and the workflow of emergency disposal of                  三防」管理實施方案》，制定「一通三防」
        accident rescue and danger. We have established a “one                  年度與月度目標，並進行考核，對未
        ventilation and three preventions” management system                    完成目標、存在「一通三防」隱患、發生
        led by the chief engineer, and set up a “one ventilation               「一通三防」責任事故和嚴重違章行為進
        and three preventions” department. The Group formulated                 行處罰。二零二二年，我們每月組織召
        annual and monthly targets for “one ventilation and three               開一次「一通三防」例會，每季度開展一
        preventions” according to the annual “one ventilation and               次「一通三防」專項檢查並組織開展礦安
        three preventions” management implementation plan, and                  全風險辨識活動。
        carried out assessments, and imposed penalties for failure
        to meet targets, the existence of “one ventilation and three
        preventions” hazards, the occurrence of “one ventilation
        and three preventions” accidents and serious violations.
        In 2022, the Group organized monthly meetings, carried
        out the quarterly special inspection on “one ventilation
        and three preventions”, and launched a mine safety risk
        identification activity.

    5.4 Developing a Culture of Safety                                   5.4 安全文化建設

       The Group is committed to cultivating a strong safety               本集團通過多元化的培訓與活動，營造
       culture through a range of training and activities. We              良好的安全文化氛圍。我們根據《煤礦
       proactively provide various types of safety education,              安全規程》，積極開展各類安全教育培
       training, and publicity in accordance with the Regulations          訓與宣傳，提升全體作業人員的技術操
       on Coal Mine Safety to enhance the technical and                    作能力。
       operational capabilities of all operators.

       During the reporting period, the Group conducted a                  報告期內，本集團採用線上、線下相結
       total of 26 workplace safety and occupational health-               合的方式，開展安全生產以及職業健康
       related training sessions both online and offline, with             等相關培訓共計26班次，培訓5,729人
       5,729 participants in attendance. In addition, the Group            次。此外，本集團開展全員安全生產責
       conducted random examinations on workplace safety                   任制抽考，並對100個工種進行安全文
       responsibilities for all employees and safety culture               化考試，參與人次達25,134，提升職工
       examinations for 100 types of work for 25,134 participants,         安全生產責任意識。

          In 2022, the Group carried out benchmarking and selection           二 零 二 二 年 ， 本 集 團 以「三 基」建 設 、「三
          activities for job workflow standards, driven by the construction   支」隊 伍 為 驅 動 ， 以 安 全 生 產 標 準 化 為 主
          of basic organization, work and capacity, and party workers,        線，開展了崗位作業流程標準對標、評選
          party group leaders and party member vanguard, and with the         活動。活動期間，我們持續推動安全生產
          standardization of workplace safety as the main line. During        「六個轉變」，嚴格落實SXFS01 – 70項作業
          the activity, we continued to promote the “six transitions”         標準和崗位作業流程標準化，為作業人員
          in workplace safety, strictly implemented SXFS01 – 70               對崗位作業流程標準化進行培訓並組織員
          operational standards and standardized job workflow, and            工進行了充分的交流探討，加深了崗位人
          provided training for operators on standardized job workflow        員對作業流程標準化的認知，促使養成「幹
          and organized sufficient exchanges and discussions among            標準活，上標準崗」與「知行合一」的良好習
          employees. This deepens their knowledge of standardized             慣，從而推動崗位作業人員流程標準常態

          of “doing standard work” and “combining learning with
          practice”, thus making the process standards for job operators

6   GREEN DEVELOPMENT                                                 6   綠色發展

    The Group is committed to the principle of green development          本集團秉承綠色發展理念，積極應對氣候變
    and is actively addressing climate change through various             化，持續提升煤基能源的清潔化生產和利用
    initiatives. It continues to enhance the clean production and         水平，大力推進節能減排和污染防治，提高
    utilization of coal-based energy, vigorously promotes energy          能源利用效率，加強綠色礦山建設和水資
    conservation, emission reduction and pollution prevention             源、生物多樣性保護工作，為建設綠色地球
    by improving energy utilization efficiency, strengthening the         家園貢獻力量。
    construction of green mines and promoting the protection of
    water resources and biodiversity, thereby contributing to the
    construction of a green home for the earth.


    6.1 Coping with climate change                                        6.1 應對氣候變化

        Against the backdrop of the national goals of “carbon               在「2030碳達峰、2060碳中和」的國家
        peaking by 2030 and carbon neutrality by 2060” and with             政策以及極端天氣頻發的背景下，本集
        more extreme weather events occurring, the Group has                團注重氣候變化管治水平提升，全面貫
        prioritized climate change governance. It fully implemented         徹國務院有關要求，支持《聯合國氣候
        the relevant requirements of the State Council to                   變化框架公約》締約方所達成的《巴黎協
                        o
        support the 2 C temperature control target of the Paris             定》2℃控溫目標。二零二二年，我們參
        Agreement reached by the parties to the United Nations              考氣候相關財務信息披露工作組(TCFD)
        Framework Convention on Climate Change by 2022.                     的框架及建議，建立了氣候變化風險管
        With reference to the framework and recommendations                 理結構，並通過調研識別了多項可能對
        of the Task Force on Climate-Related Financial Disclosures          本集團業務構成影響的機遇和實體氣候
        (TCFD), the Group has established a climate change                  風險，並通過有效措施來適應並減緩氣
        risk management structure and conducted research to                 候變化對公司業務運營和可持續發展帶
        identify a number of opportunities and physical climate             來的影響。未來我們將不斷提升應對氣
        risks that may impact its business. It has also implemented         候變化的能力，為應對全球氣候變化作
        effective measures to adapt to and mitigate the impact              出貢獻。
        of climate change on business operations and sustainable
        development. Moving forward, it will continue to enhance
        the capacity to address climate change and contribute to
        the fight against global climate change.

         The Group’s primary sources of wastewater are domestic                    本集團主要產出廢水為生活污水和礦井

         water treatment equipment for pumping and discharge                       污水處理站採用AO-MBR 3的處理工藝，
         stations to further reduce the discharge of wastewater.                   處理後的廢水全部覆用於井水防塵灑水
         In particular, the domestic wastewater treatment station                  和洗煤用水；礦井水處理站採用絮凝、
         adopts the AO-MBR 3 while the mine water treatment                        沉澱、過濾、超濾處理工藝，中水處理
         station adopts flocculation, sedimentation, filtration and                後的部分覆用於井下防塵灑水和洗煤用
         ultrafiltration, allowing all treated water for the rescue for            水，剩餘部分經活性炭過濾、超濾處理

       Corporate Clean Production Audit                                               企業清潔生產審核

       Adhered to the concept of “energy conservation,                                本集團秉承「節能、降耗、減污、增效」
       consumption reduction, pollution reduction and efficiency                      的生產理念，積極響應國家對重點行業
       enhancement”, the Group actively responds to relevant                          企業清潔生產的相關要求，鼓勵各在產
       national requirements on clean production for enterprises                      礦產探索清潔生產模式，推進使用清潔
       in key industries. The Group encourage all operational                         的能源和原料、採用先進的工藝技術與
       mines to explore clean production models, promote the                          設備、改善管理、綜合利用等措施，從
       use of clean energy and raw materials, adopt advanced                          而提高資源利用效率，減少生產過程中
       technology and equipment, and improve management and                           污染物的產生和排放，以減輕或者消除
       enable comprehensive utilization. Our goal is to improve                       對人類健康和環境的危害。
       resource utilization efficiency and reduce the generation
       and emission of pollutants in the production process, and
       ultimately reduce or eliminate the hazards to human health
       and the environment.

       The Group entrusted a professional third party to conduct                      本集團委聘專業第三方對本集團三座在

       the proposed plant site. This enables us to analyze the               目建設階段嚴格執行環境保護「三同時」
       potential impact of project construction on the surrounding           制度，確保環境保護設施與主體設施同

       into use at the same time as the main facilities, to reduce

       impact assessment, we will formulate ecological protection
       requirements for each phase of project construction
       and operation, identify ecologically sensitive targets and
       prohibited development areas based on the ecological red

       Mine Land Re-greening                                                         礦區土地復綠

       The Group will fill in the mine pits and restore the original                 我們在開採後會將礦坑填平並恢復原有
       vegetation after mining, so that the mines will be re-                        植被，讓採掘後的礦山在最短時間內重
       greened in the shortest possible time after mining. To                        披綠裝。為解決水土流失問題，我們利
       deal with soil erosion, the Group uses waste gangue to                        用廢棄的矸石打底，上層覆蓋土壤，建
       build a base, cover the upper layer with soil and establish                   立排水渠，保證水土良好融合；同時，
       drainage channels to ensure good integration of water                         我們與多家專業外部承包商合作，根據
       and soil; at the same time, it works with a number of                         礦區污染程度、地形坡度、地表物質組
       professional external contractors to lay the most suitable                    成、覆土厚度等多種因素，鋪設最適宜
       soil for the mine environment according to various factors                    礦區環境的土壤，以保證土壤肥力；
       such as the degree of pollution in the mine area, the slope                   針對礦區高寒、乾旱、水蝕、風蝕並重
       of the terrain, the composition of the surface material and                   的實際情況，礦區合理配置「草、灌、

CHAIRMAN AND MANAGING DIRECTOR                                                  主席及董事總經理

The roles of chairman and managing director are separate and                    為加強主席與董事總經理的獨立性及問責性，彼
exercised by different individuals to reinforce their independence and          等的角色已予區分，並由不同人士擔任。丁汝才
accountability. Mr. Ding Rucai is the Chairman and Mr. Fan Wenli                先生為主席，而范文利先生則為本公司的董事總
is the managing director of the Company. The Chairman provides                  經理。主席的職責負責領導董事會，確保董事會
leadership for the Board and ensures that the Board works effectively           有效地運作，且履行其職責；本公司董事總經理
and performs its responsibilities. The managing director of the                 則肩負行政總裁整體的職務，負責本集團整體的
Company has overall chief executive responsibility for the Group’s              業務發展及日常管理。主席與本公司董事總經理
business development and day-to-day management generally. The                   之間職責的分工已予清楚界定並以書面列載。
division of responsibilities between the Chairman and the managing
director of the Company is clearly established and set out in writing.

factors that may have an impact on the businesses or financial

                                               Shougang Group and/or its associates since 2015 in its usual and ordinary

                                               become connected transactions of the Company following the completion
                                               of Shoucheng’s Proposed Restructuring, on 17 May 2021, the Company
                                               and Shougang Group entered into the 2021 Master Sales and Purchase
                                               Agreement to regulate the continuing connected transactions between the
                                               Group and Shougang Group and/or its associates. The 2021 Master Sales
                                               and Purchase Agreement has become effective since 27 January 2022
                                               following the completion of Shoucheng’s Proposed Restructuring. In view
                                               of the significant increased market prices of the Fushan Products under the
                                               2021 Master Sales and Purchase Agreement, the Board expects that the
                                               original annual caps under the 2021 Master Sales and Purchase Agreement
                                               will be insufficient for the years ending 31 December 2022 and 2023.
                                               Therefore, on 23 September 2022, the Company and Shougang Group
                                               entered into the Supplemental Master Sales and Purchase Agreement to
                                               revise the annual caps and to extend the term for one year ending on 31
                                               December 2024 in order to comply with the requirements under Chapter
                                               14A of the Listing Rules.
                                               本集團於其一般及日常業務過程中自二零一零年起向首鋼集團及╱或其
                                               聯繫人供應福山產品，並自二零一五年起向首鋼集團及╱或其聯繫人購

                                               二二年一月二十七日生效。鑒於二零二一年總買賣協議項下福山產品之
                                               市場價格已大幅上漲，董事會預期，截至二零二二年及二零二三年十二

                                               十一日止，以符合上市規則第14A章項下之規定。

when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are        者根據此等綜合財務報表作出的經濟決定時，則
considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could       被視為重大錯誤陳述。
reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users
taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise                  我們根據香港審計準則進行審計的工作之一，是
professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism                運用專業判斷，在整個審計過程中抱持專業懷疑
throughout the audit. We also:                                             態度。我們亦：

•    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the         •   識別及評估綜合財務報表由於欺詐或錯誤而
     consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or                導致重大錯誤陳述的風險，因應這些風險設
     error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to those            計及執行審計程序，以及獲得充足及適當的

     to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a           詐涉及合謀串通、偽造、故意遺漏、誤導性
     material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for             陳述或凌駕內部控制，因此未能發現由此造
     one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery,        成的重大錯誤陳述風險高於未能發現由於錯
     intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of             誤而導致重大錯誤陳述的風險。
     internal control.

     2.2 Principles of consolidation and equity                              2.2 合併及權益會計原則
         accounting

          (i)   Subsidiaries                                                   (i)   附屬公司

                Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                  附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權之所
                entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                有實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集
                controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or                  團從參與實體業務而承擔取得其可
                has rights to, variable returns from its involvement                 變回報之風險或享有可變回報之權
                with the entity and has the ability to affect those                  利，並有能力透過其對實體業務之
                returns through its power to direct the activities of                主導權影響該等回報時，則本集團
                the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the             控制該實體。附屬公司於控制權轉
                date on which control is transferred to the Group.                   移至本集團當日起全面綜合入賬，
                They are deconsolidated from the date that control                   並於終止控制權當日起終止綜合入
                ceases.                                                              賬。

                The acquisition method of accounting is used to                      本集團採用收購會計法將業務合併
                account for business combinations by the Group (refer                入賬（見附註2.3）。
                to note 2.3).

                Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealised                   集團內公司間交易、結餘及集團內
                gains on transactions between group companies are                    公司間交易未變現收益均會對銷。
                eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless             未變現虧損亦會對銷，惟該交易有
                the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                   證據顯示已轉讓資產出現減值則除
                of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of                     外。附屬公司之會計政策已在有需
                subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to                    要時作出更改，以確保與本集團所
                ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the                  採納有關政策一致。
                Group.

                Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of               附屬公司業績及權益中之非控股權
                subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated                益分別於綜合損益及其他全面收益
                statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                  表、綜合權益變動表及綜合財務狀

         accounting (continued)

          (ii) Associates                                                         (ii) 聯營公司

     2.12 Financial guarantee contracts                                        2.12 財務擔保合約

          Financial guarantee contracts are recognised as a financial               財務擔保合約於出具擔保時確認為財務
          liability at the time the guarantee is issued. The liability              負債。有關負債初步按公平值計量，其
          is initially measured at fair value and subsequently at the               後則按以下較高者計量：
          higher of:

          •    the amount determined in accordance with the                         •   根據香港財務報告準則第9號項下
               expected credit loss model under HKFRS 9; and                            預期信貸虧損模式釐定之金額；及

          •    the amount initially recognised less, where                          •   初步確認之金額減根據香港財務報
               appropriate, the cumulative amount of income                             告準則第15號之原則所確認累計
               recognised in accordance with the principles of HKFRS                    收益金額（如適用）。
               15.

          The fair value of financial guarantees is determined                      財務擔保之公平值釐定為債務工具項下
          based on the present value of the difference in cash                      之合約付款與在並無擔保下將須作出
          flows between the contractual payments required under                     之付款之間之現金流量差額之現值，或
          the debt instrument and the payments that would be                        就承擔責任而將須付予第三方之估計金
          required without the guarantee, or the estimated amount                   額。
          that would be payable to a third party for assuming the
          obligations.

     2.23 Provisions                                                            2.23 撥備

          Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                   當本集團因過往事件須承擔現有法律或
          legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events,             推定責任，而解除責任將很可能需要資
          it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required             源流出，且有關金額已可作可靠估算時
          to settle the obligation and the amount can be reliably                  會確認撥備，惟不會就日後之經營虧損
          estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future                      確認任何撥備。
          operating losses.

          Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                     倘出現多項類似責任，是否需要資源流
          likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement                出以解除責任乃經考慮整體責任類別
          is determined by considering the class of obligations as a               後釐定。即使就同類責任中任何一項出
          whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an            現資源流出之可能性不大，仍會確認撥
          outflow with respect to any one item included in the same                備。
          class of obligations may be small.

          Provisions are measured at the present value of                          撥備按管理層對於報告期末預期須償付
          management’s best estimate of the expenditure required                   現有責任之支出之最佳估計之現值計
          to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting             量。用於釐定現值之貼現率為反映當時
          period. The discount rate used to determine the present                  市場對金錢時間值及負債特定風險之評
          value is a pre-tax rate that reflects current market                     估之稅前利率。隨時間流逝而增加之撥
          assessments of the time value of money and the risks                     備確認為利息開支。
          specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to
          the passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

               the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to                   之資產，且本集團有強制執行權就
               payment for performance completed to date.                            累計至今已完成之履約部分收取款
                                                                                     項。

          If control of the goods or services transfers over time,               倘貨品或服務之控制權在一段時間內轉
          revenue is recognised over the period of the contract by               移，參照在整個合約期間已完成履約
          reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction                責任之進度確認收益。否則，收益於客

          recognised at a point in time when the customer obtains                認。
          control of the goods or service.

     3.1 Financial risk factors                                                    3.1 財務風險因素

          The Group is exposed to a variety of financial risks such                     本集團面臨各種來自經營及投資活動之
          as market risk (including foreign exchange risk, cash flow                    各種財務風險，例如市場風險（包括外

          and guidelines. However, the directors meet periodically to                   而，董事定期開會分析及制訂措施以管
          analyse and formulate measures to manage the Group’s                          理本集團面對之市場風險，包括利率及
          exposure to market risks, including changes in interest rates                 匯率變動。一般而言，本集團採取保守
          and currency exchange rates. Generally, the Group employs                     之風險管理策略。由於董事認為本集團
          a conservative strategy regarding its risk management. As                     面對之市場風險維持於低水平，故本集

          risk is kept at a minimum level, the Group has not used                       進行對沖。本集團面對最重大之財務風

          (c)   Liquidity risk                                                        (c)   流動資金風險

                Liquidity risk relates to the risk that the Group will                      流動資金風險與本集團無法履行其
                not be able to meet its obligations associated with its                     財務負債相關責任之風險有關。本
                financial liabilities. The Group is exposed to liquidity                    集團面對應付貿易賬項結算及其融
                risk in respect of settlement of trade payables and                         資責任以及其現金流量管理有關之
                its financing obligations, and also in respect of its                       流動資金風險。本集團之目標為將
                cash flow management. The Group’s objective is                              流動資產及資金承擔維持於合適水
                to maintain an appropriate level of liquid assets                           平，以應付其短期及長期流動資金
                and committed lines of funding to meet its liquidity                        需求。
                requirements in the short and long term.

                The Group manages its liquidity needs by carefully                          本集團於日常業務中謹慎監察潛在
                monitoring expected payments for potential                                  投資及現金流出之預期付款，以管
                investments as well as cash-outflows due in day-to-                         理其流動資金需要。本集團按日監
                day business. Liquidity needs are monitored on a day-                       察其流動資金需要。另每月釐定為
                to-day basis. Long-term liquidity needs for a 360-day                       期360日之長期流動資金需要。
                lookout periods are identified on a monthly basis.

     percentage growth rate below.

     the license of the mining rights from the relevant authority

34. RESERVES                                                              34. 儲備

     (a) Statutory reserve                                                   (a) 法定儲備

          In accordance with the relevant laws and regulations of                 根據中國相關法律及法規，中國附屬公
          the PRC, the PRC subsidiaries are required to appropriate               司須按照中國會計規例編製提撥10%
          10% of its profit after tax, prepared in accordance with the            之除稅後溢利至法定儲備基金，直至法
          accounting regulation in the PRC, to the statutory reserve              定儲備結餘達註冊資本之50%為止。該
          fund until the statutory reserve balance reaches 50% of                 儲備可用作扣減任何虧損或撥充實繳資
          the registered capital. Such reserve may be used to reduce              本。
          any losses incurred or for capitalisation as paid-up capital.


     (b) Other reserves                                                      (b) 其他儲備

          Pursuant to the relevant PRC regulations, the Group is                  根據相關中國法規，本集團須按照產量
          required to transfer production maintenance fee, safety                 等相關基準按固定利率將維簡費、安全
          fund and other expense of similar nature (“production                   費及類似性質的其他開支（
                                                                                             「維簡基金」）
          and maintenance funds”) at fixed rates based on relevant                轉撥至專項儲備賬。維簡基金可於維簡
          bases, such as production volume, to a specific reserve                 及安全措施產生開支或資本開支時動
          account. The production and maintenance funds could                     用。已動用的維簡基金金額將從專項儲
          be utilised when expenses or capital expenditures on                    備賬轉撥至保留盈利。
          production maintenance and safety measures are incurred.
          The amount of production and maintenance funds utilised
          would be transferred from the specific reserve account to
          retained earnings.
